SUMMARY

This log documents a vibrant and unfiltered discussion within a **private digital marketing community** focused on the **OnlyFans Management (OFM)** and AI-modeling industry. The conversation centers on technical strategies for **scaling social media automation**, specifically through the use of **cloud phones, mobile proxies**, and specialized software like GeeLark to avoid platform bans. Members actively exchange "sauce"—highly valued insider knowledge—regarding **AI content generation**, debating the effectiveness of various **LoRA models and video workflows** to create realistic virtual influencers. Beyond technical troubleshooting, the dialogue reveals a **pragmatic and often cynical culture** where participants share tactics for "mother/slave" marketing and the psychological conversion of followers into paying subscribers.

Keywords: Instagram Marketing Strategies, AI Content Creation, Cloud Phone Management, Proxy Server Setup, Adult Model Automation
